    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01J7RCD3CbqYYsHaLCJrMEqK`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01FDYWRGaguuBLNaqB5HyoVf`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	name: on-experiment-failed
3	description: 实验失败后的自动诊断工作流——查日志分析原因、更新 registry、通知 Director。[CRON] 实验失败消息到达时使用。
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	- workflow
7	- diagnosis
8	- cron-hook
9	tools:
10	- read_registry
11	- tail_training_logs
12	- update_registry
13	- append_idea
14	- feishu_send_result_card
15	- spawn_worker
16	- check_job_status
17	version: 4
18	heavy: true
19	roles:
20	- agent
21	---
22	
23	# 实验失败诊断工作流
24	
25	## 触发方式
26	由 cron hook 自动触发：进程退出且 W&B 无数据 / W&B state=failed/crashed / NaN 检测到。
27	
28	## 步骤
29	
30	### Step 0: 读取项目上下文（诊断前必须执行）
31	先读文档，再看日志——否则诊断方向可能完全错误：
32	
33	- `read_project_file("memory.md")` — **最重要**：查是否有相同失败模式的历史记录和已知修复方案
34	- Worker 通过 `list_repo_tree` + `read_repo_file` 按需从代码仓库读取训练脚本结构
35	
36	读完后检查：
37	- memory.md 里有没有"此类实验曾失败，原因是 X"的记录？→ 直接按已知方案诊断，跳过 Step 2 通用流程
38	- 代码仓库中的训练入口和参数格式是否与本次实验配置匹配？（Worker 可用 `list_repo_tree` + `read_repo_file` 查看）
39	
40	### Step 1: 了解失败背景
41	- read_registry(exp_id) — 查看状态、conclusion、notes、metrics、log_path
42	
43	### Step 2: 浅层日志诊断
44	有 log_path 时：tail_training_logs(server, **绝对路径**, lines=80)
45	- 寻找：OOM、CUDA error、NaN、assertion error、数据加载错误、Python Traceback
46	- 没找到 → 增加行数再查
47	
48	### Step 2.5: 深查（满足以下任一条件时 spawn_worker）
49	- 运行时长 < 5 分钟（初始化阶段崩溃）
50	- tail 日志中未见明确 Traceback 或错误行
51	- 同类实验连续失败 ≥ 2 次
52	
53	```python
54	spawn_worker(
55	    task_type="diagnose:{exp_id}",
56	    task_prompt="""SSH 到服务器 {server}，诊断实验 {exp_id} 的失败原因。
57	
58	项目路径：{project_path}（绝对路径）
59	日志路径：{log_path}（可能为空）
60	
61	执行步骤（按顺序，有结论就停）：
62	
63	1. 如果有日志路径：
64	   - 先看结尾 50 行（找 Traceback / Error / CUDA OOM）
65	   - 没找到明确错误：再看开头 100 行（初始化崩溃在这里）
66	   - 日志文件不存在：说明任务启动即失败，跳到步骤 2
67	
68	2. 日志无错误或无日志时，检查基础设施：
69	   - 入口文件是否存在
70	   - 激活环境并验证 import
71	   - 检查 configs/ 目录
72	
73	3. 检查磁盘/内存（OOM 类）
74	
75	最后输出一行 JSON：
76	{"root_cause":"<引用具体报错行>","error_type":"infrastructure|hyperparameter|resource|data","fix_steps":["步骤1","步骤2"],"can_auto_fix":true}""",
77	    tool_groups=["ssh"],
78	)
79	```
80	
81	Worker 返回后，以其结论作为 Step 3 的诊断依据。
82	
83	### Step 3: 诊断 + 行动
84	根据 `error_type` 决定：
85	- **infrastructure**（文件缺失/import error/配置语法）→ spawn_worker 修复，修复后重提交（自主修复最多 1 次，再失败则停止告知用户）。注意：此重试策略仅适用于训练实验，数据生成任务的重试策略参见 `on-datagen-done` 的 Step 9。
86	  - spawn_worker 后立即：`update_registry(exp_id, "notes", "fix_worker_id: {worker_id}")`，确保关联可追溯
87	- **hyperparameter/架构**（loss 不降、指标差）→ 给具体调整建议，用户决定
88	- **resource**（OOM、GPU 不够）→ 给配置调整建议，用户确认
89	- **data**（路径、格式）→ spawn_worker 检查，能自修则修，不能则告知用户
90	
91	### Step 4: 更新 registry
92	- update_registry(exp_id, "notes", "失败原因: ... | 已有成果: ... | 建议: ...")
93	- update_registry(exp_id, "conclusion", "failed")
94	- update_registry(exp_id, "status", "DONE")
95	
96	### Step 5: 发送飞书结果卡片
97	- feishu_send_result_card(exp_id, conclusion="failed", key_metrics, baseline_comparison)
98	  - key_metrics：从 registry 取已有 metrics，没有则传空 dict
99	  - baseline_comparison：Step 3 的诊断摘要
100	
101	### Step 6: 提炼 insight + 更新文档
102	
103	**写入 memory.md（新失败模式才写，避免重复）**：
104	如果 root_cause 是之前未记录的模式（Step 0 读 memory.md 时没有看到），用 `save_memory` 追加：
105	```
106	- [失败模式] {error_type}：{root_cause 一句话摘要} → 修复方案：{fix_steps 摘要}
107	```
108	例：`- [失败模式] GPU 硬件：GPU 7 CUDA capability 检查失败，device=7, num_gpus=7 → 设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 跳过 GPU 7`
109	这样下次同类实验失败时，Step 0 读 memory.md 就能直接命中，跳过通用诊断流程。
110	
111	**更新 ideas.md（有价值的 insight 才写）**：
112	- AMP 崩溃反复出现 → `append_idea` 记录调参方向
113	- 部分 metrics 有价值但后期崩溃 → `append_idea` 记录调优方向
114	
115	更新 ideas.md IN_PROGRESS → DEAD ENDS：
116	- move_idea(from_section="IN_PROGRESS（进行中）", to_section="DEAD ENDS（已确认死路）", index=N)
117	- 找不到对应条目：跳过
118	
119	### Step 6.5: 更新 report.md
120	
121	report.md 由系统根据 registry 状态自动生成，无需手动调用 `save_project_file`。Step 4 更新 registry 后，report 会自动刷新并在 ## 告警 区块突出标注本次失败。
122	
123	### Step 7: 回复
124	生成失败报告：exp_id、失败原因（引用具体错误行）、已有成果、具体建议。
125	
````

bound\\\\\\\"，不能 claim universal。与 exp-013 cross-transfer \\\\\\\"self-familiarity effect\\\\\\\" 一致。\\\\n\\\\n### K*(neural probe) 模型相关 [2026-05-15, exp-015 v4 verified]\\\\nPythia K*_neural=2, OLMo K*_neural=2, Qwen-cont K*_neural=3。Qwen-cont 升高源于 self-familiarity effect（instruction-tuned model 对自身输出的 hidden state 有更丰富的 mode-specific artifacts）。15D hand-crafted features K*=2 universal，更稳健可迁移。\\\\n\\\\n### exp-015 GPU OOM 教训 [2026-05-14]\\\\nexp-006 (15.5GB) + exp-015 (~9GB) 超出 4090D 24GB 显存。**硬规则**: 后续 GPU 并行必须用 `torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()` 报告 peak VRAM，不用 nvidia-smi 静态快照（低估 peak usage）。GPU 共享实验必须独占，不并行。\\\\n\\\\n### Rebuttal 实验汇总 [2026-05-15]\\\\n- EXP-A (FR-2 Critical): Cross-scorer K*∈{1,2}，按 generator data 分裂。ρ≥0.90 确认 feature ranking stable。\\\\n- EXP-B (FR-3 Critical): 14D (去 self_BLEU) K*=2 stable across all models。\\\\n- EXP-C (FR-11): 10K permutation, all boundary p<0.0001。\\\\n- EXP-D (FR-12): OBD N=500 accuracy=0.380 [0.340, 0.424] > LLMs (0.332/0.331)。\\\\n- EXP-E (FR-5): Holm-Bonferroni 10/15 MMLU significant, binary-ours significantly best。\\\\n\\\\n## 经验\\\\n\\\\n### AutoDL / westc-26893\\\\n- ModelScope CDN 下载 HuggingFace 模型比直连快（国内网络）\\\\n- source /etc/network_turbo 启用代理加速\\\\n- HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1 避免 xet_get 401 错误\\\\n- SSL 证书需设 REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE + SSL_CERT_FILE\\\\n- conda 环境：source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\\\\n- 服务器别名从 autodl-4090d 改为 westc-26893（exp-003 cron 失败教训）\\\\n\\\\n### 实验管理\\\\n- submit_training_job 必须用于后台训练（不用 nohup）——自动 tmux + cron 监控\\\\n- Registry 状态必须顺序推进：PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → DONE\\\\n- GPU 编号始终从 0 开始（框架自动映射物理 GPU）\\\\n- **exp-006 实际速度 ~45.2min/run**（training 37.5min + eval 7.7min, range 44-49min）。99min 估计是错误的，monitor Worker 从实际 run 时间戳验证 45.2min 正确。\\\\n- exp-006 lm-eval 配置：batch_size=\\\\\\\"auto\\\\\\\", device=cuda:0, **MMLU 0-shot + HellaSwag 0-shot**（lm-eval 默认 0），eval 仅占 17% 总耗时 [2026-05-14]\\\\n- **exp-006 实际目录结构**：输出在 `outputs/exp006/`（非 `runs/exp006_graduated_filtering/`）。6 策略：`no-filter, binary-ours, binary-drayson, graduated, surprisal, dsir`。graduated 用 α∈{0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9} sweep。eval 结果格式：`scores.mmlu[\\\\\\\"acc,none\\\\\\\"]`, `scores.hellaswag[\\\\\\\"acc_norm,none\\\\\\\"]` [2026-05-14]\\\\n\\\\n### 论文写作\\\\n- Claude proxy model ID: {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_7/projects/gen-depth-contamination/literature_map.yaml\", \"content\": \"papers:\\n  # === Must-Cite ===\\n\\n  - id: drayson2025\\n    title: \\\"Machine-generated text detection prevents language model collapse\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Drayson, Yilmaz, Lampos\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"EMNLP 2025\\\"\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15654\\\"\\n    code: \\\"https://github.com/GeorgeDrayson/model_collapse\\\"\\n    category: \\\"verification-curation\\\"\\n    role: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"Binary MGT detection + importance resampling for collapse prevention; our depth estimator generalizes their binary detector to ordinal depth\\\"\\n    cited: false\\n    methodology: >\\n      Binary MGT detector (encoder-only transformer + classification head; best: ModernBERT-150M, AUC .986 in-dist, .943 OOD).\\n      Fine-tuned 5 epochs with label smoothing α=0.1 + temperature scaling.\\n      Sampling Importance Resampling (SIR): w_j = (1-q(x_j))^b / Σ(1-q(x_l))^b, where q(x) = P(machine-generated), b≥1 bias term.\\n      Resample at k=1.5 (50% upsampling). Evaluated on GPT-2 and SmolLM2 (124M–1.7B).\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"SIR filtering not only prevents collapse but improves over human-only training (perplexity -0.85% to -25.65%, diversity +3.44% to +14.62%, MAUVE +8.46% to +20.92%)\\\"\\n      - \\\"ModernBERT achieves best detection (AUC .986 in-dist); optimal threshold 0.8674 on imbalanced data (29% human)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Curated synthetic data has positive value — outperforms oracle (perfect filter) in equal-ratio settings\\\"\\n      - \\\"Only evaluates fine-tuning (not pretraining), models ≤2B, open-ended generation only\\\"\\n    positioning_notes: >\\n      Closest prior work. Their SIR is a special case of our graduated filtering when K*=1 (binary: human vs all synthetic).\\n      We generalize binary→ordinal: estimate depth d∈{0,1,...,K*} and apply depth-graduated weights.\\n      K* tells when ordinal signal adds value over binary. They never discuss ordinal depth or multi-class estimation.\\n      Our C3 claim directly extends their framework: \\\"even coarse 3-class signal yields better calibration at decision boundaries.\\\"\\n\\n  - id: schaeffer2025position\\n    title: \\\"Position: Model Collapse Does Not Mean What You Think\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Schaeffer, Kazdan, Arulandu, Koyejo\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICML 2025\\\"\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03150\\\"\\n    category: \\\"model-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"framing-critical\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"Identifies 8 definitions of model collapse across 3 families; forces us to position K* as discriminability saturation (distribution deformation), not test-loss collapse\\\"\\n    cited: false\\n    methodology: >\\n      Position paper. Surveys model collapse literature and identifies 8 distinct definitions organized in 3 families.\\n      Evaluates each definition against realistic deployment conditions. No experiments—purely analytical/argumentative.\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"8 definitions in 3 families: (A) Population risk degrading [D1: catastrophic increase, D2: any increase, D3: asymptotic divergence]; (B) Distributions deforming [D4: collapsing variance, D5: scaling law change, D6: mode disappearance/entanglement, D7: tail data loss]; (C) Spurious generation [D8: hallucinated data]\\\"\\n      - \\\"Under realistic conditions (data accumulation, growing compute), catastrophic risk divergence (D1-D3) is avoidable\\\"\\n      - \\\"Real harms—tail data loss (D7) and mode entanglement (D6)—have received disproportionately less attention\\\"\\n      - \\\"Recommends researchers explicitly state which definition they study\\\"\\n    positioning_notes: >\\n      K* belongs to Family B (distributions deforming), specifically D4 (collapsing variance) + D6 (mode entanglement).\\n      K* measures when distribution deformation renders generations indistinguishable in feature space.\\n      K* is NOT about population risk (Family A). This distinction is critical for framing:\\n      we contribute a diagnostic for exactly the harms Schaeffer et al. identify as underexplored.\\n      Adopt their taxonomy explicitly in Related Work to anchor K* precisely.\\n\\n  - id: kazdan2025collapse\\n    title: \\\"Collapse or Thrive? Perils and Promises of Synthetic Data in a Self-Generating World\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Kazdan, Schaeffer, Dey, Gerstgrasser, Rafailov, Donoho, Koyejo\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICML 2025\\\"\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16713\\\"\\n    category: \\\"model-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"Accumulate vs replace regimes affect contamination dynamics; in accumulate regime K* may shift upward\\\"\\n    cited: false\\n    methodology: >\\n      Formal comparison of 3 data regimes: Replace (train on latest synthetic only; Σ→0 a.s.),\\n      Accumulate (train on all historical data; real fraction→0 but stability holds),\\n      Accumulate-subsample (fixed budget from accumulated pool; intermediate degradation).\\n      Theory: Gaussian setting with closed-form limits. Experiments: multivariate Gaussian, KDE, LLM SFT (Gemma2-2B on HelpSteer2/TinyStories).\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"Replace: covariance collapses to 0 (a.s.), W2 diverges. Collapse inevitable within 5-10 iterations\\\"\\n      - \\\"Accumulate: covariance stabilizes at σ₀²·sin(π/√n)/(π/√n). No collapse even as real data fraction→0\\\"\\n      - \\\"Accumulate-subsample: degradation plateaus rather than diverges\\\"\\n      - \\\"Cardinality of real data matters more than proportion (R²=0.59 vs 0.34)\\\"\\n    positioning_notes: >\\n      Our prompt-chain = replace regime (harshest setting). K*=2 measured under replace conditions.\\n      This makes K*=2 a conservative lower bound: under accumulate regime (more realistic), K* would shift upward.\\n      Strengthens our result: if depth estimation adds value at K*=2 under replace, it adds more under accumulate.\\n      Cite to justify experimental design and contextualize K* as regime-dependent.\\n\\n  - id: shi2025generalization\\n    title: \\\"A Closer Look at Model Collapse: From a Generalization-to-Memorization Perspective\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Shi, Wu, Zhang, Zhang, Tao, Qu\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"NeurIPS 2025 Spotlight\\\"\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16499\\\"\\n    code: \\\"https://shilianghe007.github.io/model-collapse/\\\"\\n    category: \\\"model-collapse\\\"\\n    ro\\n\\n... No experiments—purely analytical/argumentative.\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"8 definitions in 3 families: (A) Population risk degrading [D1: catastrophic increase, D2: any increase, D3: asymptotic divergence]; (B) Distributions deforming [D4: collapsing variance, D5: scaling law change, D6: mode disappearance/entanglement, D7: tail data loss]; (C) Spurious generation [D8: hallucinated data]\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Under realistic conditions (data accumulation, growing compute), catastrophic risk divergence (D1-D3) is avoidable\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Real harms—tail data loss (D7) and mode entanglement (D6)—have received disproportionately less attention\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Recommends researchers explicitly state which definition they study\\\\\\\"\\\\n    positioning_notes: >\\\\n      K* belongs to Family B (distributions deforming), specifically D4 (collapsing variance) + D6 (mode entanglement).\\\\n      K* measures when distribution deformation renders generations indistinguishable in feature space.\\\\n      K* is NOT about population risk (Family A). Now I'm looking at the feature importance patterns across different model scales and architectures. I'm also noticing this pattern holds across Qwen variants and OLMo, suggesting it might be a meaningful scale-dependent phenomenon worth documenting in the experiment reflection and literature review.

For positioning against prior work, our 7B results extend beyond Drayson et al.'s evaluation range up to 1.7B, so we can claim validation of the K*=2 finding at a larger scale. This also connects to Schaeffer and Kazdan's theoretical frameworks about distribution deformation and collapse dynamics in the replace regime. I'm ready to draft the Director report and set up the next experiment batch while integrating these findings into the paper.

  "text": "[资源] GPU 分配 1 / 实际跑 0 ⚠️ (exp-004 刚完成, exp-012 待启动) | Worker 池 1 (GPU 0 / CPU 1 诊断)\n\n## exp-004 Cross-Scale 7B 实验 — ✅ 成功\n\n**结果**: K*=2 在 Qwen2.5-7B (14.5GB, fp16) 下确认成立，FR-4 Warning 解决。\n\n### 指标对比\n\n| 模型 | 规模 | AUC 0v1 | AUC 1v2 | AUC 2v3 | K* |\n|------|------|---------|---------|---------|-----|\n| Qwen-RW (mvp) | 1.5B | 0.996 | 0.696 | 0.589 | 2 |\n| Pythia | 1.4B | 0.993 | 0.654 | 0.552 | 2 |\n| OLMo | 1B | 0.997 | 0.615 | 0.550 | 2 |\n| Qwen-cont | 1.5B | 0.997 | 0.611 | 0.561 | 2 |\n| **Qwen-7B** | **7B** | **0.964** | **0.618** | **0.546** | **2** |\n\n### 关键发现\n1. **K*=2 scale-invariant**: 从 1B 到 7B 全部一致，可以在论文中 claim \"K*=2 holds across model scales (1B–7B)\"\n2. **0v1 AUC 下降** (0.964 vs >0.99): 更大模型生成更 human-like 文本，human/machine 边界更模糊——这是符合预期的趋势，可写入 Discussion\n3. **1v2 仍 >θ=0.60** (0.618): 虽然 0v1 下降了，但 generational depth 的 K*=2 边界不变\n4. **Feature importance**: freq_entropy 在 7B 首次超过 surprisal 成为 top-1，与 Qwen 家族一致\n\n### 审稿问题关闭状态\n- FR-1 θ理论依据 ✅ | FR-2 scorer混淆 ✅ | FR-3 self_BLEU ✅ \n- FR-4 ≤1.5B规模 ✅ (本实验) | FR-5 多重比较 ✅ | FR-11 permutation ✅ | FR-12 LLM N=500 ✅\n- **全部 3 Critical + 所有 Warning 已解决**\n\n### 实验计划反思\n- exp-004 成功 → C1' claim 可升级表述: \"K*=2 is scale-invariant across 1B–7B, 4 model families, 4 decoding strategies\"\n- 最后一个实验 exp-012 (binary confidence vs depth) 已 QUEUED，代码 dry-run 验证过，预计 60-90 min\n- 论文整合：exp-004 结果需写入 experiments.tex + discussion.tex（更大模型 0v1 下降趋势）\n\n### 文献联动\n- vs Drayson et al. (EMNLP 2025): 他们仅评估 ≤1.7B，我们 7B 结果扩展了 scale 验证范围\n- Schaeffer 2025 taxonomy: K* 作为 distribution deformation diagnostic 在更大 scale 仍有效\n\n### 请求\n1. **请分配 exp_012_binary_confidence 到 westc-26893:cuda:0**（GPU 现已空闲）\n2. 分配后我立即提交，预计 05-16 ~01:00 UTC 完成\n3. 完成后启动 Paper Agent 整合最后两个实验结果",
  "project": "gen-depth-contamination"
}
